theorem IndependentDuodenary.image_double_zero.{u_1, u_2} : ∀ {K : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} [inst : AddMonoid K]
  [inst_1 : AddMonoid B], (∀ (x : K), x + x = 0) → ∀ (f : K →+ B) (x : K), f x + f x = 0 :=
fun {K} {B} [AddMonoid K] [AddMonoid B] hK f x =>
  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (Eq.symm (map_add f x x))))
    (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => f _a = 0) (hK x)))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (map_zero f))) (Eq.refl 0)))
'IndependentDuodenary.image_double_zero' does not depend on any axioms
DIRECT DEPENDENCIES image_double_zero: [Eq.mpr,
 AddMonoidHom.instAddMonoidHomClass,
 AddMonoid.toAddSemigroup,
 congrArg,
 AddMonoid.toAddZeroClass,
 AddMonoid.toZero,
 map_zero,
 map_add,
 id,
 instHAdd,
 AddSemigroup.toAdd,
 HAdd.hAdd,
 AddMonoidHomClass.toAddHomClass,
 AddMonoid,
 Zero.toOfNat0,
 Eq.refl,
 AddMonoidHomClass.toZeroHomClass,

 AddMonoidHom.instFunLike,
 OfNat.ofNat,
 Eq.symm,
 Eq,
 DFunLike.coe]

theorem IndependentDuodenary.binaryCoordinate_not_injective : ∀ {ℓ : ℕ} (i j : Fin ℓ),
  i ≠ j → ¬Function.Injective ⇑(IndependentDuodenary.binaryCoordinate i) :=
fun {ℓ} i j hij h =>
  let v := fun k => if k = j then 1 else 0;
  let_fun hv := of_eq_true (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) (ite_cond_eq_false 1 0 (eq_false hij))) (eq_self 0));
  let_fun heq := h hv;
  let_fun hj := congrFun heq j;
  False.elim
    (Eq.mp (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) (ite_cond_eq_true 1 0 (eq_self j))) Mathlib.Algebra.NeZero._auxLemma.3)
      hj)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve_range.{u_1} : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : AddMonoid K] (χ : K →+ ZMod 2),
  Function.Surjective ⇑χ → ∀ (y : ZMod 12), (∃ x, (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) x = y) ↔ y = 0 ∨ y = 6 :=
fun {K} [AddMonoid K] χ hχ y =>
  { mp := fun a => Exists.casesOn a fun x h => h ▸ of_decide_eq_true (id (Eq.refl true)) (χ x),
    mpr := fun hy =>
      Or.casesOn hy
        (fun h =>
          Eq.symm h ▸
            Exists.casesOn (hχ 0) fun x hx =>
              Exists.intro x
                (let_fun this :=
                  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve _a = 0) hx))
                    IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve_values.left;
                this))
        fun h =>
        Eq.symm h ▸
          Exists.casesOn (hχ 1) fun x hx =>
            Exists.intro x
              (let_fun this :=
                Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve _a = 6) hx))
                  IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve_values.right;
              this) }

  Function.Injective ⇑(IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) ↔ Function.Injective ⇑χ :=

  {

            (id (congrArg (fun _a => IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve _a = IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve (χ y)) hxy))
            (Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve (χ y)));
        this),
    mpr := fun h => Function.Injective.comp IndependentDuodenary.bitToTwelve_injective h }

theorem IndependentDuodenary.no_binary_ring_hom_twelve : ¬Nonempty (ZMod 2 →+* ZMod 12) :=
fun a =>
  Nonempty.casesOn a fun f =>
    let_fun h :=
      Trans.trans
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 1 + 1 = _a) (map_add f 1 1)))
          (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 1 + 1 = _a + _a) (map_one f))) (Eq.refl (1 + 1))))
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => f _a = 0) (IndependentDuodenary.binary_double_zero 1)))
          (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (map_zero f))) (Eq.refl 0)));
    of_decide_eq_true (id (Eq.refl true)) h

theorem IndependentDuodenary.maximum_weight_iff : ∀ (v : IndependentDuodenary.BinaryVector),
  IndependentDuodenary.bitWeight v = 12 ↔ ∀ (i : Fin 12), v i = 1 :=
fun v =>
  {
    mp := fun h i =>
      let_fun hs := Finset.eq_univ_of_card (IndependentDuodenary.support v) h;
      let_fun hi := Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => i ∈ _a) hs)) (Finset.mem_univ i);
      IndependentDuodenary.binary_nonzero_eq_one (v i) (Finset.mem_filter.mp hi).right,
    mpr := fun h =>
      let_fun hs :=
        Finset.ext fun i =>
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans
              (congr
                (congrArg Iff
                  (Eq.trans Mathlib.Data.Finset.Filter._auxLemma.2
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congr (congrArg And (Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Basic._auxLemma.1 i))
                        (Eq.trans
                          (congrArg Not (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) (h i)) Mathlib.Algebra.NeZero._auxLemma.3))
                          not_false_eq_true))
                      (and_self True))))
                (Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Basic._auxLemma.1 i))
              (iff_self True));
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 12) (Eq.trans (congrArg Finset.card hs) (Fintype.card_fin 12)))
          (eq_self 12)) }

theorem IndependentDuodenary.weightLayer_card : ∀ (k : ℕ),
  (IndependentDuodenary.weightLayer k).card = Nat.choose 12 k :=
fun k =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg (fun _a => (IndependentDuodenary.weightLayer k).card = _a.choose k)
        (Eq.symm
          (let_fun this := rfl;
          this))))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id (congrArg (fun _a => (IndependentDuodenary.weightLayer k).card = _a.choose k) (Eq.symm Finset.card_univ)))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg (fun _a => (IndependentDuodenary.weightLayer k).card = _a)
            (Eq.symm (Finset.card_powersetCard k Finset.univ))))
        (Finset.card_bij (fun v x => IndependentDuodenary.support v)
          (fun v hv =>
            Eq.mpr (id IndependentDuodenary.BinaryStructure._auxLemma.1)
              ⟨Finset.subset_univ (IndependentDuodenary.support v), (Finset.mem_filter.mp hv).right⟩)
          (fun v hv w hw heq =>
            funext fun i =>
              let_fun hi :=
                let_fun hm := Finset.ext_iff.mp heq i;
                id
                  (Eq.mp
                    (congr
                      (congrArg Iff
                        (Eq.trans Mathlib.Data.Finset.Filter._auxLemma.2
                          (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x ∧ ¬v i = 0) (Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Basic._auxLemma.1 i))
                            (true_and ¬v i = 0))))
                      (Eq.trans Mathlib.Data.Finset.Filter._auxLemma.2
                        (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x ∧ ¬w i = 0) (Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Basic._auxLemma.1 i))
                          (true_and ¬w i = 0))))
                    hm);
              if h : v i = 0 then
                let_fun hw0 := Decidable.byContradiction fun hn => hi.mpr hn h;
                Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = w i) h))
                  (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 0 = _a) hw0)) (Eq.refl 0))
              else
                Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = w i) (IndependentDuodenary.binary_nonzero_eq_one (v i) h)))
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id (congrArg (fun _a => 1 = _a) (IndependentDuodenary.binary_nonzero_eq_one (w i) (hi.mp h))))
                    (Eq.refl 1)))
          fun s hs =>
          let v := fun i => if i ∈ s then 1 else 0;
          let_fun hv :=
            Finset.ext fun i =>
              of_eq_true
                (Eq.trans
                  (congrArg (fun x => i ∈ x ↔ i ∈ s)
                    (Eq.trans
                      ((fun {α} p p_1 e_p =>
                          @Eq.rec (α → Prop) p
                            (fun p_2 e_p =>
                              ∀ {inst : DecidablePred p} [inst_1 : DecidablePred p_2] (s s_1 : Finset α),
                                s = s_1 → Finset.filter p s = Finset.filter p_2 s_1)
                            (fun {inst} [inst_1 : DecidablePred p] =>
                              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun [DecidablePred p] =>
                                ∀ (s s_1 : Finset α), s = s_1 → Finset.filter p s = Finset.filter p s_1)
                                (fun s s_1 e_s => e_s ▸ Eq.refl (Finset.filter p s)) (Subsingleton.elim inst inst_1))
                            p_1 e_p)
                        (fun i => v i ≠ 0) (fun i => i ∈ s)
                        (funext fun i =>
                          Eq.trans
                            (congrArg Not
                              (Eq.trans Init.PropLemmas._auxLemma.66
                                (Eq.trans (implies_congr (Eq.refl (i ∈ s)) Mathlib.Algebra.NeZero._auxLemma.3)
                                  Init.Core._auxLemma.5)))
                            Init.PropLemmas._auxLemma.52)
                        Finset.univ Finset.univ (Eq.refl Finset.univ))
                      (Finset.filter_univ_mem s)))
                  (iff_self (i ∈ s)));
          Exists.intro v
            (Exists.intro
              (Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (Eq.trans IndependentDuodenary.BinaryStructure._auxLemma.2
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congrArg (fun x => x ∧ IndependentDuodenary.bitWeight v = k)
                        (IndependentDuodenary.BinaryStructure._auxLemma.3 v))
                      (true_and (IndependentDuodenary.bitWeight v = k)))))
                (let_fun this :=
                  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a.card = k) hv)) (Finset.mem_powersetCard.mp hs).right;
                this))
              hv))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.conditional_closure.{u_1, u_2} : ∀ {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} (sourceOp : A → A → A)
  (targetOp : B → B → B) (f : A → B) (S : Set A) (T : Set B),
  IndependentDuodenary.PreservesOperation sourceOp targetOp f →
    (∀ x ∈ S, ∀ y ∈ S, sourceOp x y ∈ S) → (∀ (b : B), b ∈ T ↔ ∃ a ∈ S, f a = b) → ∀ x ∈ T, ∀ y ∈ T, targetOp x y ∈ T :=
fun {A} {B} sourceOp targetOp f S T hpres hclosed himage x hx y hy =>
  Exists.casesOn ((himage x).mp hx) fun a h =>
    And.casesOn h fun ha right =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun x => x ∈ T → targetOp x y ∈ T)
        (fun hx =>
          Exists.casesOn ((himage y).mp hy) fun b h =>
            And.casesOn h fun hb right =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun y => y ∈ T → targetOp (f a) y ∈ T)
                (fun hy =>
                  (himage (targetOp (f a) (f b))).mpr (Exists.intro (sourceOp a b) ⟨hclosed a ha b hb, hpres a b⟩))
                right hy)
        right hx
'IndependentDuodenary.conditional_closure' does not depend on any axioms
DIRECT DEPENDENCIES conditional_closure: [Iff.mpr,
 Membership.mem,
 Exists,
 IndependentDuodenary.PreservesOperation,
 And.casesOn,
 And,
 Iff,
 Exists.casesOn,
 And.intro,
 Exists.intro,
 Iff.mp,
 Eq.ndrec,
 Eq,

theorem IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock_pullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} [inst : Zero V] [inst_1 : DecidableEq V] (b : IndependentDuodenary.FullBlock E X V),
  IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock b) = b :=
fun {E} {X} {V} [Zero V] [DecidableEq V] b =>
  IndependentDuodenary.FullBlock.casesOn b fun e a d =>
    if ha : a = 0 then
      if hd : d = 0 then
        if had : a = d then
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans
              (congr
                (congrArg
                  (fun x =>
                    Eq
                      (match x with
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := v, secondSlope := 0 }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := v }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := v, secondSlope := v }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := 0 }))
                  (Eq.trans
                    (ite_cond_eq_true
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                      else
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                      else
                        if
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                        else
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) (id (Eq.mp (congrArg (Eq a) hd) had))) (eq_self 0)))
                    (ite_cond_eq_true
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin)
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                        IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) hd) (eq_self 0)))))
                (congr (congrArg (IndependentDuodenary.FullBlock.mk e) (id (Eq.mp (congrArg (Eq a) hd) had))) hd))
              (eq_self { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := 0 }))
        else
          False.elim
            (Eq.mp (Eq.trans (congrArg Not (Eq.trans (congr (congrArg Eq ha) hd) (eq_self 0))) not_true_eq_false) had)
      else
        if had : a = d then False.elim (Eq.mp (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) had) (eq_false hd)) ha)
        else
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans
              (congr
                (congrArg
                  (fun x =>
                    Eq
                      (match x with
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := v, secondSlope := 0 }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := v }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := v, secondSlope := v }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := 0 }))
                  (Eq.trans
                    (ite_cond_eq_true
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                      else
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                      else
                        if
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                        else
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) ha) (eq_self 0)))
                    (ite_cond_eq_false
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin)
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                        IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (eq_false hd))))
                (congrArg (fun x => { origin := e, firstSlope := x, secondSlope := d }) ha))
              (eq_self { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := d }))
    else
      if hd : d = 0 then
        if had : a = d then
          False.elim
            (Eq.mp
              (Eq.trans (congrArg Not (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) (Eq.trans had hd)) (eq_self 0)))
                not_true_eq_false)
              ha)
        else
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans
              (congr
                (congrArg
                  (fun x =>
                    Eq
                      (match x with
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := v, secondSlope := 0 }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := v }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third v =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := v, secondSlope := v }
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e =>
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := 0, secondSlope := 0 }))
                  (Eq.trans
                    (ite_cond_eq_false
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                      else
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                      else
                        if
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                        else
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (eq_false ha))
                    (ite_cond_eq_true
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                        IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope)
                      (if
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton

              (eq_self { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := 0 }))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock_correction.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} [inst : Zero V] [inst_1 : DecidableEq V] (b : IndependentDuodenary.FullBlock E X V),
  IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock b) =
    (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock b).2 :=
fun {E} {X} {V} [Zero V] [DecidableEq V] b =>
  IndependentDuodenary.FullBlock.casesOn b fun e a d =>
    if ha : a = 0 then
      if hd : d = 0 then
        if had : a = d then
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans
              (congr
                (congrArg
                  (fun x =>
                    Eq
                      (match x with
                      | IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third v =>
                        IndependentDuodenary.parityCorrection e v
                      | x => 0))
                  (Eq.trans
                    (ite_cond_eq_true
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                      else
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.first
                          { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                      else
                        if
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.third
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                        else
                          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope)
                      (Eq.trans (congrArg (fun x => x = 0) (id (Eq.mp (congrArg (Eq a) hd) had))) (eq_self 0)))
                    (ite_cond_eq_true
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin)
                      (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton
                        { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                        IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.second

                        (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.omitted, 0)
                      else
                        (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.secondVariable
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope,
                          0))
                      (if { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope = 0 then
                        (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.firstVariable
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope,
                          0)
                      else
                        if
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                          (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.singleton
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.thirdVariable
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope,
                            IndependentDuodenary.parityCorrection
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope)
                        else
                          (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.equation
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.occurrence
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope,
                            0))
                      (eq_false ha))
                    (Eq.trans
                      (ite_cond_eq_false
                        (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.firstVariable
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope,
                          0)
                        (if
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope =
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope then
                          (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.singleton
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.thirdVariable
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope,
                            IndependentDuodenary.parityCorrection
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope)
                        else
                          (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.equation
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.occurrence
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                              { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope,
                            0))
                        (eq_false hd))
                      (ite_cond_eq_false
                        (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.singleton
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.thirdVariable
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope,
                          IndependentDuodenary.parityCorrection
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope)
                        (IndependentDuodenary.ReducedBlock.equation
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.origin.occurrence
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.firstSlope
                            { origin := e, firstSlope := a, secondSlope := d }.secondSlope,
                          0)
                        (eq_false had))))))
              (eq_self 0))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.pullback_reducedSourceTable.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : DecidableEq V],
  (∀ (v : V), v + v = 0) →
    ∀ (t : IndependentDuodenary.FullTable k E X V),
      IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) = t :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [DecidableEq V] hDouble t =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun x =>
          { intercept := IndependentDuodenary.fullReducedIntercept t + x,
              blocks := fun i =>
                IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock (t.blocks i)) } =
            t)
        (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
          Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock (t.blocks x))))))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun x =>
            {
                intercept :=
                  IndependentDuodenary.fullReducedIntercept t +
                    ∑ x : Fin k,
                      IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock (t.blocks x)),
                blocks := x } =
              t)
          (funext fun i => IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock_pullback (t.blocks i))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun x =>
              { intercept := IndependentDuodenary.fullReducedIntercept t + x, blocks := fun i => t.blocks i } = t)
            (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
              IndependentDuodenary.sourceSupportBlock_correction (t.blocks x))))
        (IndependentDuodenary.FullTable.casesOn (motive := fun t_1 =>
          t = t_1 →
            {
                intercept :=
                  IndependentDuodenary.fullReducedIntercept t +
                    ∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (t.blocks x)).2,
                blocks := fun i => t.blocks i } =
              t)
          t
          (fun intercept blocks h =>
            Eq.symm h ▸
              of_eq_true
                (Eq.trans
                  (congrArg
                    (fun x =>
                      { intercept := x, blocks := fun i => blocks i } = { intercept := intercept, blocks := blocks })
                    (Eq.trans
                      (Eq.trans
                        (congr
                          (congrArg (fun x => HAdd.hAdd (intercept + x))
                            (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                              Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (blocks x)).2))
                          (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                            Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (blocks x)).2))
                        (add_assoc intercept (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (blocks x)).2)
                          (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (blocks x)).2)))
                      (Eq.trans
                        (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd intercept)
                          ((fun v => hDouble v) (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (blocks x)).2)))
                        (add_zero intercept))))
                  (eq_self { intercept := intercept, blocks := fun i => blocks i })))
          (Eq.refl t))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_double_zero.{u_3} : ∀ {V : Type u_3} [inst : AddCommMonoid V]
  [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V] (v : V), v + v = 0 :=
fun {V} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] v =>
  Trans.trans
    (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => v + v = _a) (add_smul 1 1 v)))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => v + v = _a + _a) (one_smul (ZMod 2) v))) (Eq.refl (v + v))))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (fun _a => _a • v = 0)
          (let_fun this := of_decide_eq_true (id (Eq.refl true));
          this)))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (zero_smul (ZMod 2) v))) (Eq.refl 0)))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_add_eq_zero_iff.{u_3} : ∀ {V : Type u_3} [inst : AddCommMonoid V]
  [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V] (a d : V), a + d = 0 ↔ a = d :=
fun {V} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] a d =>
  {
    mp := fun h =>
      let_fun heq := congrArg (fun v => v + d) h;
      Eq.mp
        (congr
          (congrArg Eq
            (Eq.trans (add_assoc a d d)
              (Eq.trans (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd a) (IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_double_zero d)) (add_zero a))))
          (zero_add d))
        heq,
    mpr := fun a_1 => a_1 ▸ IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_double_zero a }

                            (Lean.Data.AC.Context.eq_of_norm
                              { op := HAdd.hAdd, assoc := AddSemigroup.to_isAssociative,
                                comm := some { down := AddCommMagma.to_isCommutative }, idem := none,
                                vars :=
                                  [{ value := x, neutral := none }, { value := x_1, neutral := none },
                                    { value := x_2, neutral := none }, { value := x_3, neutral := none }],
                                arbitrary := x }
                              (((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1)).op
                                ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 3)))
                              ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0).op
                                ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1).op ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 3))))
                              (Eq.refl true)))
                        (x • a) (y • d) (u • a) (v • d))))
                  (Eq.refl (x • a + (y • d + (u • a + v • d))))))
              (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a + (u • a + v • d) = 0) h))
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_double_zero (u • a + v • d))))
                  (Eq.refl 0)));
          And.casesOn (hk (x + u) (y + v) hz) fun hx hy =>
            Prod.ext ((IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_add_eq_zero_iff x u).mp hx)
              ((IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_add_eq_zero_iff y v).mp hy))
        h

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_reduction_factorization.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V] [inst_2 : DecidableEq V]
  (t : IndependentDuodenary.FullTable k E X V) (p : ZMod 2 × (Fin k → ZMod 2 × ZMod 2)),
  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullTable t p =
    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t)
      (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p) :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [DecidableEq V] t p =>
  let_fun h := IndependentDuodenary.source_table_pullback_evaluation (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p;
  Eq.mp
    (congrArg
      (fun _a =>
        IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullTable _a p =
          IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t)
            (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p))
      (IndependentDuodenary.pullback_reducedSourceTable IndependentDuodenary.binary_module_double_zero t))
    h

  Function.Surjective (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t)) :=

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_reduction_unique.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3}
  {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V] [inst_2 : DecidableEq V]
  (t : IndependentDuodenary.FullTable k E X V)
  (g :
    ZMod 2 ×
        ((i : Fin k) →
          IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates ((IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t).blocks i)) →
      V),
  (∀ (p : ZMod 2 × (Fin k → ZMod 2 × ZMod 2)),
      g (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p) =
        IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullTable t p) →
    g = IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [DecidableEq V] t g hg =>
  funext fun z =>
    Exists.casesOn (IndependentDuodenary.source_reduction_projection_surjective t z) fun p h =>
      h ▸
        Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                _a =
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t)
                    (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p))
              (hg p)))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  _a =
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t)
                      (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p))
                (IndependentDuodenary.source_reduction_factorization t p)))
            (Eq.refl
              (IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t)
                (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection (IndependentDuodenary.reducedSourceTable t) p))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.translateKey_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {M : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {W : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddZeroClass K] (q : IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey M K W), IndependentDuodenary.translateKey 0 q = q :=
fun {M} {K} {W} [AddZeroClass K] q =>
  IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.casesOn (motive := fun t => q = t → IndependentDuodenary.translateKey 0 q = q) q
    (fun metadata binaryIntercept complementIntercept h =>
      Eq.symm h ▸
        of_eq_true
          (Eq.trans
            (congrArg
              (fun x =>
                { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := x, complementIntercept := complementIntercept } =
                  { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                    complementIntercept := complementIntercept })
              (add_zero binaryIntercept))
            (eq_self
              { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                complementIntercept := complementIntercept })))
    (Eq.refl q)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.translateKey_add.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {M : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {W : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddSemigroup K] (h g : K) (q : IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey M K W),
  IndependentDuodenary.translateKey g (IndependentDuodenary.translateKey h q) =
    IndependentDuodenary.translateKey (h + g) q :=
fun {M} {K} {W} [AddSemigroup K] h g q =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congrArg
        (fun x =>
          { metadata := q.metadata, binaryIntercept := x, complementIntercept := q.complementIntercept } =
            { metadata := q.metadata, binaryIntercept := q.binaryIntercept + (h + g),
              complementIntercept := q.complementIntercept })
        (add_assoc q.binaryIntercept h g))
      (eq_self
        { metadata := q.metadata, binaryIntercept := q.binaryIntercept + (h + g),
          complementIntercept := q.complementIntercept }))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.keyOrbit_iff_vertex_eq.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {M : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {W : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddGroup K] (q r : IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey M K W),
  IndependentDuodenary.SameKeyOrbit q r ↔ IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q = IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r :=
fun {M} {K} {W} [AddGroup K] q r =>
  { mp := fun a => Exists.casesOn a fun h h_1 => h_1 ▸ Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q),
    mpr := fun h =>
      let_fun hm := congrArg Prod.fst h;
      let_fun hw := congrArg Prod.snd h;
      Exists.intro (-q.binaryIntercept + r.binaryIntercept)
        (IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
          q = t → IndependentDuodenary.translateKey (-q.binaryIntercept + r.binaryIntercept) q = r) q
          (fun metadata binaryIntercept complementIntercept h_1 =>
            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun q =>
              IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q = IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r →
                (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q).1 = (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).1 →
                  (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q).2 = (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).2 →
                    IndependentDuodenary.translateKey (-q.binaryIntercept + r.binaryIntercept) q = r)
              (fun h hm hw =>
                IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
                  r = t →
                    IndependentDuodenary.translateKey
                        (-{ metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                complementIntercept := complementIntercept }.binaryIntercept +
                          r.binaryIntercept)
                        { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                          complementIntercept := complementIntercept } =
                      r)
                  r
                  (fun metadata_1 binaryIntercept_1 complementIntercept_1 h_2 =>
                    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun r =>
                      IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex
                            { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                              complementIntercept := complementIntercept } =
                          IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r →
                        (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex
                                { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                  complementIntercept := complementIntercept }).1 =
                            (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).1 →
                          (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex
                                  { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                    complementIntercept := complementIntercept }).2 =
                              (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).2 →
                            IndependentDuodenary.translateKey
                                (-{ metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                        complementIntercept := complementIntercept }.binaryIntercept +
                                  r.binaryIntercept)
                                { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                  complementIntercept := complementIntercept } =
                              r)
                      (fun h hm hw =>
                        of_eq_true
                          (Eq.trans
                            (congrArg
                              (fun x =>
                                x =
                                  { metadata := metadata_1, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept_1,
                                    complementIntercept := complementIntercept_1 })
                              (congr
                                (congr (congrArg IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.mk hm)
                                  (Eq.trans
                                    (IndependentDuodenary.SourceKeys._auxLemma.1 binaryIntercept (-binaryIntercept)
                                      binaryIntercept_1)
                                    (Eq.trans
                                      (congrArg (fun x => x + binaryIntercept_1) (add_neg_cancel binaryIntercept))
                                      (zero_add binaryIntercept_1))))
                                hw))
                            (eq_self
                              { metadata := metadata_1, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept_1,
                                complementIntercept := complementIntercept_1 })))
                      (Eq.symm h_2) h hm hw)
                  (Eq.refl r))
              (Eq.symm h_1) h hm hw)
          (Eq.refl q)) }

theorem IndependentDuodenary.canonicalKey_unique.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {M : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {W : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddGroup K] (q r : IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey M K W),
  IndependentDuodenary.SameKeyOrbit q r → r.binaryIntercept = 0 → r = IndependentDuodenary.canonicalKey q :=
fun {M} {K} {W} [AddGroup K] q r horbit hz =>
  let_fun hv := (IndependentDuodenary.keyOrbit_iff_vertex_eq q r).mp horbit;
  let_fun hm := congrArg Prod.fst hv;
  let_fun hw := congrArg Prod.snd hv;
  IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.casesOn (motive := fun t => q = t → r = IndependentDuodenary.canonicalKey q) q
    (fun metadata binaryIntercept complementIntercept h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun q =>
        IndependentDuodenary.SameKeyOrbit q r →
          IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q = IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r →
            (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q).1 = (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).1 →
              (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex q).2 = (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).2 →
                r = IndependentDuodenary.canonicalKey q)
        (fun horbit hv hm hw =>
          IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
            r = t →
              r =
                IndependentDuodenary.canonicalKey
                  { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                    complementIntercept := complementIntercept })
            r
            (fun metadata_1 binaryIntercept_1 complementIntercept_1 h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun r =>
                r.binaryIntercept = 0 →
                  IndependentDuodenary.SameKeyOrbit
                      { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                        complementIntercept := complementIntercept }
                      r →
                    IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex
                          { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                            complementIntercept := complementIntercept } =
                        IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r →
                      (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex
                              { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                complementIntercept := complementIntercept }).1 =
                          (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).1 →
                        (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex
                                { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                  complementIntercept := complementIntercept }).2 =
                            (IndependentDuodenary.keyVertex r).2 →
                          r =
                            IndependentDuodenary.canonicalKey
                              { metadata := metadata, binaryIntercept := binaryIntercept,
                                complementIntercept := complementIntercept })
                (fun hz horbit hv hm hw =>
                  of_eq_true
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congr
                        (congrArg
                          (fun x =>
                            Eq
                              { metadata := metadata_1, binaryIntercept := x,
                                complementIntercept := complementIntercept_1 })
                          (id (id hz)))
                        (congr (congrArg (fun x => IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.mk x 0) hm) hw))
                      (eq_self
                        { metadata := metadata_1, binaryIntercept := 0,
                          complementIntercept := complementIntercept_1 })))
                (Eq.symm h) hz horbit hv hm hw)
            (Eq.refl r))
        (Eq.symm h) horbit hv hm hw)
    (Eq.refl q)

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_folding.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {M : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {W : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddSemigroup K] (label : M × W → K) (h : K) (q : IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey M K W),
  IndependentDuodenary.restoredAnswer label (IndependentDuodenary.translateKey h q) =
    IndependentDuodenary.restoredAnswer label q + h :=
fun {M} {K} {W} [AddSemigroup K] label h q =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congrArg (Eq (label (q.metadata, q.complementIntercept) + (q.binaryIntercept + h)))
        (add_assoc (label (q.metadata, q.complementIntercept)) q.binaryIntercept h))
      (eq_self (label (q.metadata, q.complementIntercept) + (q.binaryIntercept + h))))

  q = r → IndependentDuodenary.restoredAnswer label q = IndependentDuodenary.restoredAnswer label r :=

theorem IndependentDuodenary.residue_folding_twelve.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {M : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {W : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddMonoid K] (χ : K →+ ZMod 2) (label : M × W → ZMod 12) (h : K) (q : IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey M K W),
  IndependentDuodenary.residueAnswerTwelve χ label (IndependentDuodenary.translateKey h q) =
    IndependentDuodenary.residueAnswerTwelve χ label q + (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h :=
fun {M} {K} {W} [AddMonoid K] χ label h q =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congr
        (congrArg (fun x => Eq (label (q.metadata, q.complementIntercept) + x))
          (map_add (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) q.binaryIntercept h))
        (add_assoc (label (q.metadata, q.complementIntercept)) ((IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) q.binaryIntercept)
          ((IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h)))
      (eq_self
        (label (q.metadata, q.complementIntercept) +
          ((IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) q.binaryIntercept + (IndependentDuodenary.bridgeTwelve χ) h))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection_lift.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3}
  (b : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock E X V) (t₀ : ZMod 2) (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z :=
fun {E} {X} {V} b t₀ z =>
  IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
    b = t → IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z) b
    (fun e a d h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
        ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
          IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z)
        (fun z =>
          Eq.refl
            (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d) t₀
              (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.retained e a d) t₀ z)))
        (Eq.symm h) z)
    (fun e p v h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
        ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
          IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z)
        (fun z =>
          IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
            p = t →
              IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                  (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀ z) =
                z)
            p
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)),
                  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                        z) =
                    z)
                (fun z => of_eq_true (eq_self z)) (Eq.symm h) z)
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)),
                  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                        z) =
                    z)
                (fun z => of_eq_true (eq_self z)) (Eq.symm h) z)
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
                ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)),
                  IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                        z) =
                    z)
                (fun z =>
                  of_eq_true
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congrArg (fun x => x = z)
                        (Eq.trans
                          (congr
                            (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                              (Eq.trans
                                (Eq.trans
                                  (congrArg (fun x => (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0) + (x, 0).1)
                                    (sub_eq_add_neg z (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0)))
                                  (add_left_comm (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0) z
                                    (-if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0)))
                                (Eq.trans (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd z) (add_neg_cancel (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0)))
                                  (add_zero z))))
                            (congrArg (fun x => (x, 0).2) (sub_eq_add_neg z (if e.parity = true then t₀ else 0))))
                          (add_zero z)))
                      (eq_self z)))
                (Eq.symm h) z)
            (Eq.refl p))
        (Eq.symm h) z)
    (fun e h =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
        ∀ (z : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedCoordinates b),
          IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift b t₀ z) = z)
        (fun z =>
          PUnit.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
            z = t →
              IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀
                  (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀ z) =
                z)
            z
            (fun h =>
              Eq.symm h ▸
                Eq.refl
                  (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀
                    (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockLift (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.omitted e) t₀
                      PUnit.unit)))
            (Eq.refl z))
        (Eq.symm h) z)
    (Eq.refl b)

  Function.Surjective (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection t) :=

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_block_pullback_evaluation.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V] (b : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock E X V)
  (t₀ : ZMod 2) (xy : ZMod 2 × ZMod 2),
  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock b) xy +
      t₀ • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection b =
    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock b (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ xy) :=
fun {E} {X} {V} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] b t₀ xy =>
  IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
    b = t →
      IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock b) xy +
          t₀ • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection b =
        IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock b (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection b t₀ xy))
    b
    (fun e a d h =>
      Eq.symm h ▸
        of_eq_true
          (Eq.trans
            (congrArg (fun x => x = xy.1 • a + xy.2 • d)
              (Eq.trans (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (xy.1 • a + xy.2 • d)) (smul_zero t₀)) (add_zero (xy.1 • a + xy.2 • d))))
            (eq_self (xy.1 • a + xy.2 • d))))
    (fun e p v h =>
      Eq.symm h ▸
        IndependentDuodenary.SourcePosition.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
          p = t →
            IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock
                  (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)) xy +
                t₀ • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) =
              IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v)
                (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock.singleton e p v) t₀
                  xy))
          p
          (fun h =>
            Eq.symm h ▸
              Bool.casesOn (motive := fun t =>
                e.parity = t →
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock
                        (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_table_pullback_evaluation.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {V : Type u_3} {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : Module (ZMod 2) V]
  (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X V) (p : ZMod 2 × (Fin k → ZMod 2 × ZMod 2)),
  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullTable (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t) p =
    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedTable t (IndependentDuodenary.permittedProjection t p) :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] t p =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congr
        (congrArg
          (fun x => Eq (p.1 • (t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i)) + x))
          (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
            Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x))))
        (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (p.1 • t.intercept))
          (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
            Eq.refl
              (IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)
                (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks x) p.1 (p.2 x)))))))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            _a +
                ∑ x : Fin k,
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x) =
              p.1 • t.intercept +
                ∑ x : Fin k,
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)
                    (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks x) p.1 (p.2 x)))
          (smul_add p.1 t.intercept (∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i)))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              p.1 • t.intercept + _a +
                  ∑ x : Fin k,
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x) =
                p.1 • t.intercept +
                  ∑ x : Fin k,
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)
                      (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks x) p.1 (p.2 x)))
            Finset.smul_sum))
        (let_fun hs :=
          Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  _a =
                    ∑ i : Fin k,
                      IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)
                        (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks i) p.1 (p.2 i)))
                (Eq.symm Finset.sum_add_distrib)))
            (Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i a =>
              IndependentDuodenary.source_block_pullback_evaluation (t.blocks i) p.1 (p.2 i));
        Trans.trans
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congr
                (congrArg Eq
                  ((fun x x_1 x_2 =>
                      id
                        (Lean.Data.AC.Context.eq_of_norm
                          { op := HAdd.hAdd, assoc := AddSemigroup.to_isAssociative,
                            comm := some { down := AddCommMagma.to_isCommutative }, idem := none,
                            vars :=
                              [{ value := x, neutral := none }, { value := x_1, neutral := none },
                                { value := x_2, neutral := none }],
                            arbitrary := x }
                          (((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0)).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1))
                          ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0).op ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2)))
                          (Eq.refl true)))
                    (∑ x : Fin k, p.1 • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks x))
                    (∑ x : Fin k,
                      IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x))
                    (p.1 • t.intercept)))
                ((fun x x_1 x_2 =>
                    id
                      (Lean.Data.AC.Context.eq_of_norm
                        { op := HAdd.hAdd, assoc := AddSemigroup.to_isAssociative,
                          comm := some { down := AddCommMagma.to_isCommutative }, idem := none,
                          vars :=
                            [{ value := x, neutral := none }, { value := x_1, neutral := none },
                              { value := x_2, neutral := none }],
                          arbitrary := x }
                        ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2).op ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0)))
                        ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 0).op ((Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 1).op (Lean.Data.AC.Expr.var 2)))
                        (Eq.refl true)))
                  (∑ i : Fin k, p.1 • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i))
                  (∑ x : Fin k,
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x))
                  (p.1 • t.intercept))))
            (Eq.refl
              (∑ x : Fin k, p.1 • IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks x) +
                (∑ x : Fin k,
                    IndependentDuodenary.evaluateFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x)) (p.2 x) +
                  p.1 • t.intercept))))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  p.1 • t.intercept + _a =
                    p.1 • t.intercept +
                      ∑ x : Fin k,
                        IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)
                          (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks x) p.1 (p.2 x)))
                hs))
            (Eq.refl
              (p.1 • t.intercept +
                ∑ i : Fin k,
                  IndependentDuodenary.evaluateProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)
                    (IndependentDuodenary.sourceBlockProjection (t.blocks i) p.1 (p.2 i))))))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.pullback_block_metadata.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3}
  [inst : AddZeroClass V] [inst_1 : DecidableEq V] (b : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedBlock E X V),

theorem IndependentDuodenary.pullback_reduced_intercept.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {V : Type u_3}
  {k : ℕ} [inst : AddCommMonoid V] [inst_1 : DecidableEq V],
  (∀ (v : V), v + v = 0) →
    ∀ (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X V),
      IndependentDuodenary.fullReducedIntercept (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t) =
        IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t :=
fun {E} {X} {V} {k} [AddCommMonoid V] [DecidableEq V] hDouble t =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun x =>
          t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i) + x =
            t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
        (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
          Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x))).2)))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (fun _a => _a = t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
          (add_assoc t.intercept (∑ i : Fin k, IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks i))
            (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x))).2))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              t.intercept + _a = t.intercept + ∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
            (Eq.symm Finset.sum_add_distrib)))
        ((fun {α β γ} [HAdd α β γ] a a_1 e_a =>
            Eq.rec (motive := fun a_2 e_a => ∀ (a_3 a_4 : β), a_3 = a_4 → a + a_3 = a_2 + a_4)
              (fun a_2 a_3 e_a => e_a ▸ Eq.refl (a + a_2)) e_a)
          t.intercept t.intercept (Eq.refl t.intercept)
          (∑ x : Fin k,
            (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackCorrection (t.blocks x) +
              (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackBlock (t.blocks x))).2))
          (∑ i : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).2)
          (Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i a => IndependentDuodenary.pullback_block_intercept hDouble (t.blocks i)))))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.permitted_pullback_exact_key.{u_1, u_2, u_4, u_5} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2} {k : ℕ}
  {K : Type u_4} {W : Type u_5} [inst : AddCommMonoid K] [inst_1 : AddCommMonoid W] [inst_2 : DecidableEq K]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq W],
  (∀ (v : K × W), v + v = 0) →
    ∀ (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X (K × W)),
      IndependentDuodenary.fullExactKey (IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t) =
        IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey t :=
fun {E} {X} {k} {K} {W} [AddCommMonoid K] [AddCommMonoid W] [DecidableEq K] [DecidableEq W] hDouble t =>
  let_fun hi := IndependentDuodenary.pullback_reduced_intercept hDouble t;
  let_fun hm := funext fun i => IndependentDuodenary.pullback_block_metadata (t.blocks i);
  id
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            {
                metadata := fun i =>
                  (IndependentDuodenary.reduceFullBlock ((IndependentDuodenary.pullbackTable t).blocks i)).1,
                binaryIntercept := _a.1, complementIntercept := _a.2 } =
              { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
                binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
                complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 })
          hi))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              { metadata := _a, binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
                  complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 } =
                { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
                  binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
                  complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 })
            hm))
        (Eq.refl
          { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
            binaryIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).1,
            complementIntercept := (IndependentDuodenary.projectedReducedIntercept t).2 })))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.projected_exact_key_translate.{u_1, u_2, u_4, u_5} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
  {k : ℕ} {K : Type u_4} {W : Type u_5} [inst : AddCommMonoid K] [inst_1 : AddCommMonoid W] [inst_2 : DecidableEq K]
  [inst_3 : DecidableEq W] (h : K) (t : IndependentDuodenary.ProjectedTable k E X (K × W)),
  IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey (IndependentDuodenary.translateProjectedTable h t) =
    IndependentDuodenary.translateKey h (IndependentDuodenary.projectedExactKey t) :=
fun {E} {X} {k} {K} {W} [AddCommMonoid K] [AddCommMonoid W] [DecidableEq K] [DecidableEq W] h t =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans
      (congr
        (congrArg Eq
          (congr
            (congrArg
              (IndependentDuodenary.ExactKey.mk fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1)
              (Eq.trans
                (congrArg Prod.fst
                  (Eq.trans
                    (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (t.intercept + (h, 0)))
                      (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                        Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))
                    (add_assoc t.intercept (h, 0)
                      (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))))
                (add_left_comm t.intercept.1 h
                  (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).1)))
            (Eq.trans
              (congrArg Prod.snd
                (Eq.trans
                  (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (t.intercept + (h, 0)))
                    (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                      Eq.refl (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))
                  (add_assoc t.intercept (h, 0)
                    (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2))))
              (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd t.intercept.2)
                (zero_add (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).2)))))
        (congrArg
          (fun x =>
            { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1, binaryIntercept := x,
              complementIntercept :=
                t.intercept.2 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).2 })
          (add_comm (t.intercept.1 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).1) h)))
      (eq_self
        { metadata := fun i => (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks i)).1,
          binaryIntercept :=
            h + (t.intercept.1 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).1),
          complementIntercept :=
            t.intercept.2 + (∑ x : Fin k, (IndependentDuodenary.reduceProjectedBlock (t.blocks x)).2).2 }))

theorem IndependentDuodenary.source_canonical_key_representable.{u_1, u_2, u_4, u_5} : ∀ {E : Type u_1} {X : Type u_2}
